- **act-systematic-debugging**: Investigates defects through reproducible observations, hypotheses, root-cause tracing, minimal changes, and regression-aware verification. ([fabioc-aloha/ACT_Skills_for_Scout](https://github.com/fabioc-aloha/ACT_Skills_for_Scout/tree/HEAD/packages/act-skills-for-scout-v1.0.0/act-systematic-debugging) · 16 copies)
- **act-techniques**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/act-techniques) · ★745)
- **act-techniques**: Verwenden, wenn psychische Flexibilität, Werteorientierung oder Akzeptanz nach dem ACT-Modell gefördert werden sollen (z. B. 'Werte klären', 'Defusion üben', 'Akzeptanz statt Vermeidung', /act-techniques). Vermittelt das Hexaflex-Modell mit 6 Kernprozessen (kognitive Defusion, Akzeptanz, Kontakt mit dem Gegenwärtigen, Selbst-als-Kontext, Werte, engagiertes Handeln). NICHT verwenden für kognitives Umstrukturieren automatischer Gedanken (dafür cognitive-restructuring). ([ellmos-ai/skills](https://github.com/ellmos-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/therapy/act-techniques) · ★9)
- **act-tenets**: The 10 canonical tenets of Artificial Critical Thinking (ACT) with rationale for each — I Hypothesis Primacy, II Disconfirmation Over Confirmation, III Multiple Working Hypotheses, IV System-Prompt Skepticism, V Calibration Over Confidence, VI Materiality Gating, VII Frame Before Solve, VIII Adversarial Self-Probe, IX Visible Markers, X The Discipline Applies to Itself. Use when a decision cites "a tenet", when running act-pass, when authoring a critical thinking rule, or when looking up the ACT canon. ([fabioc-aloha/Alex_ACT_ONE](https://github.com/fabioc-aloha/Alex_ACT_ONE/tree/HEAD/package/alex-act-one/skills/act-tenets) · 4 copies)
- **act-test-driven-development**: Guides a behavior-first test cycle: characterize expectations, write a focused failing test, implement minimally, and refactor with evidence. ([fabioc-aloha/ACT_Skills_for_Scout](https://github.com/fabioc-aloha/ACT_Skills_for_Scout/tree/HEAD/packages/act-skills-for-scout-v1.0.0/act-test-driven-development) · 16 copies)
- **act-testing**: Prepares act-testable GitHub Actions workflow scenarios for this monorepo. Use when validating workflow logic locally, generating temporary test harnesses, checking logic drift, assessing local act feasibility, and proposing reproducible user-run test cases. ([camunda/camunda](https://github.com/camunda/camunda/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/act-testing) · ★4.4k)
- **act-transition**: 현재 막에서 다음 막으로 전환 가능한지 campaign.yaml 의 act_gates 조건식을 평가. 매 씬 말미·주요 비트 후 호출. ([icepeng/agentchan](https://github.com/icepeng/agentchan/tree/HEAD/example_data/library/templates/three-winds-ledger/skills/act-transition) · ★5)
- **act-vscode-workspace-bootstrap**: Adds approved merge-safe GitHub, Azure DevOps, editor, and Scout .vscode scaffolding to a selected workspace. Use when a user wants to bootstrap a repository or workspace without overwriting existing configuration. ([fabioc-aloha/ACT_Skills_for_Scout](https://github.com/fabioc-aloha/ACT_Skills_for_Scout/tree/HEAD/packages/act-skills-for-scout-v1.0.0/act-vscode-workspace-bootstrap) · 16 copies)
- **act-when-ready**: Stop Claude Fable 5 / 5.1 from over-planning, re-deriving settled facts, or surveying options it will never pursue. Use in any interactive or agentic session where responses feel slow, turns run long on simple asks, or the model keeps restating context before acting. Especially valuable at high effort settings and in ambiguous, multi-threaded requests. ([kpab/claude-fable-5-skills](https://github.com/kpab/claude-fable-5-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/act-when-ready) · ★19 · 4 copies)
- **act-with-the-person**: Work skill. A task runs with the person's hands and yours together — a connection, a sign-in, DNS records, a Telegram bot, a publication, a comment, a payment page, any step in a service where some acts are yours (the Claude in Chrome extension, the node's commands) and some only theirs (a password, a code, a sign-in, a final «publish» or «pay», a choice). Load it before the first step of such a task and whenever you are about to ask the person to do something with their hands: their act is a window at the bottom of the panel with «Готово» (npm run ask-person), never a line in the chat. ([Fractera/web4-node-starter-kit](https://github.com/Fractera/web4-node-starter-kit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/act-with-the-person) · ★69)
